1. **Identify the black nodes:** E and Q.
2. **Find the connections of node E:** E is connected to F, B, and S.
3. **Find the connections of node Q:** Q is connected to I, A, and J.
4. **Determine the shortest path from E to Q:**
   - From E, potential paths are via F, B, or S.
   - From Q, potential paths are via I, A, or J.

Now, let's explore the paths:

- **Path via B:** E -> B -> Q is not possible because B is not directly connected to Q.
- **Path via F:** F is connected to B, C, and T. None of these directly lead to Q.
- **Path via S:** S is connected to B and A. From A, we can reach Q.

Thus, the shortest path is:
E -> S -> A -> Q

Length: 3, Path: [E, S, A, Q]

- From B, we can go to C, G, or H.
- From G, we can go to I.
- From I, we can go to J or Q.
- Finally, from Q, we can go to R.
